# main.py — LumenForge MR Star LED Remote
import sys
import os
import asyncio
import sqlite3
import datetime
import re
import json
import queue
import time
import traceback
import threading
import math
import colorsys

from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QApplication, QMainWindow, QTabWidget, QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout,
    QPushButton, QListWidget, QListWidgetItem, QLabel, QComboBox, QLineEdit,
    QCheckBox, QTableWidget, QTableWidgetItem, QMessageBox, QGridLayout,
    QSlider, QGroupBox, QInputDialog, QScrollArea, QTextEdit, QPlainTextEdit,
    QSpinBox, QFileDialog, QMenu, QSizePolicy
)
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, QTimer, QRectF, QPointF, Signal
from PySide6.QtGui import (
    QAction, QIcon, QPainter, QColor, QPixmap, QConicalGradient,
    QRadialGradient, QPen, QBrush, QFont
)
from bleak import BleakScanner, BleakClient
import qasync

from mrstar_protocol import (
    MRSTAR_SERVICE_UUID,
    MRSTAR_WRITE_CHAR_UUID,
    CMD_ON, CMD_OFF, CMD_STATIC,
    SCROLL_MACRO,
    encode_mrstar_power,
    encode_mrstar_static,
    encode_mrstar_brightness,
    encode_mrstar_color,
    encode_classic_magic_home_on,
    encode_classic_magic_home_off,
    encode_classic_magic_home_color,
    decode_hex_command
)

sd = None
np = None
AUDIO_TRIED = False
AUDIO_OK = True

def load_audio():
    global sd, np, AUDIO_TRIED, AUDIO_OK
    if AUDIO_TRIED:
        return AUDIO_OK
    AUDIO_TRIED = True
    try:
        import sounddevice as _sd
        import numpy as _np
        sd = _sd
        np = _np
        AUDIO_OK = True
    except Exception as e:
        log_error(f"audio import failed: {e}")
        AUDIO_OK = False
    return AUDIO_OK

APP_NAME = "LumenForge"
APP_VERSION = "v1.3"
LOG_FILE = "lumenforge_errors.log"
ICON_NAMES = ["AymashTain.ico", "aymashtaine.ico", "lumenforge.ico", "icon.ico"]
BRIGHTNESS_MAX = 900
BRIGHTNESS_MIN = 100

_events = queue.Queue(maxsize=5000)

def emit(kind, msg):
    try:
        _events.put_nowait((datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%H:%M:%S"), kind, msg))
    except queue.Full:
        pass

def app_dir():
    if getattr(sys, "frozen", False):
        return os.path.dirname(sys.executable)
    return os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))

def log_error(msg):
    try:
        path = os.path.join(app_dir(), LOG_FILE)
        if os.path.exists(path) and os.path.getsize(path) > 2 * 1024 * 1024:
            try:
                os.replace(path, path + ".old")
            except Exception:
                pass
        ts = datetime.datetime.now().isoformat(timespec="seconds")
        with open(path, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(f"[{ts}] {msg}\n")
        emit("error", msg)
    except Exception:
        pass

def excepthook(exc_type, exc_value, exc_tb):
    text = "".join(traceback.format_exception(exc_type, exc_value, exc_tb))
    log_error("UNCAUGHT:\n" + text)
    try:
        QMessageBox.critical(None, "AymashTain", "Error logged; app continues.")
    except Exception:
        pass

def find_icon():
    d = app_dir()
    try:
        files = os.listdir(d)
    except Exception as e:
        log_error(f"Icon: cannot list {d}: {e}")
        return None
    for name in ICON_NAMES:
        if name in files:
            return os.path.join(d, name)
    lower = {f.lower(): f for f in files}
    for name in ICON_NAMES:
        if name.lower() in lower:
            return os.path.join(d, lower[name.lower()])
    icos = [f for f in files if f.lower().endswith(".ico")]
    if icos:
        return os.path.join(d, icos[0])
    return None


class DB:
    def __init__(self, path=None):
        if path is None:
            path = os.path.join(app_dir(), "lumenforge.db")
        self.conn = sqlite3.connect(path)
        self.conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        self._init()

    def _init(self):
        c = self.conn.cursor()
        c.execute("""CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS profiles (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, name TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
            protocol TEXT DEFAULT 'MRStar', created_at TEXT NOT NULL)""")
        c.execute("""CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS commands (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, ts TEXT NOT NULL,
            device_address TEXT, char_uuid TEXT, hex TEXT NOT NULL,
            label TEXT, notes TEXT)""")
        c.execute("""CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS remote_buttons (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, profile_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
            label TEXT NOT NULL, hex TEXT NOT NULL, char_uuid TEXT,
            group_name TEXT DEFAULT 'Ungrouped', sort_order INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
            is_macro INTEGER DEFAULT 0, macro_frames TEXT,
            macro_delay_ms INTEGER DEFAULT 60,
            FOREIGN KEY(profile_id) REFERENCES profiles(id))""")
        c.execute("""CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS devices (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, address TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
            name TEXT, last_char_uuid TEXT, last_seen TEXT)""")
        self.conn.commit()
        if not self.get_profiles():
            self._seed()

    def _seed(self):
        pid = self.create_profile("Default", "MRStar")
        seed = [
            ("Power", "Turn ON", encode_mrstar_power(True)),
            ("Power", "Turn OFF", encode_mrstar_power(False)),
            ("Power", "Static", encode_mrstar_static()),
            ("Colors", "White", encode_mrstar_color(255, 255, 255, brightness=1.0)),
            ("Colors", "Red", encode_mrstar_color(255, 0, 0, brightness=1.0)),
            ("Colors", "Green", encode_mrstar_color(0, 255, 0, brightness=1.0)),
            ("Colors", "Blue", encode_mrstar_color(0, 0, 255, brightness=1.0)),
            ("Colors", "Yellow", encode_mrstar_color(255, 255, 0, brightness=1.0)),
            ("Colors", "Cyan", encode_mrstar_color(0, 255, 255, brightness=1.0)),
            ("Colors", "Magenta", encode_mrstar_color(255, 0, 255, brightness=1.0)),
            ("Effects", "M-strip blank", "BC060200500055"),
            ("Effects", "M-strip white", "BC060200640055"),
            ("Effects", "M-crawl", "BC060200C80055"),
        ]
        for i, (g, l, h) in enumerate(seed):
            self.add_remote_button(pid, l, h, None, g, i)
        self.add_remote_button(
            pid, "Scroll Music (locks)", SCROLL_MACRO[0], None, "Macros", 0,
            is_macro=1, macro_frames=json.dumps(SCROLL_MACRO), macro_delay_ms=55)

    def get_profiles(self):
        return self.conn.execute("SELECT * FROM profiles ORDER BY name").fetchall()

    def get_profile(self, name):
        return self.conn.execute("SELECT * FROM profiles WHERE name=?", (name,)).fetchone()

    def get_profile_id(self, name):
        r = self.get_profile(name)
        return r["id"] if r else None

    def create_profile(self, name, protocol="MRStar"):
        ts = datetime.datetime.now().isoformat(timespec="seconds")
        self.conn.execute("INSERT INTO profiles (name, protocol, created_at) VALUES (?,?,?)",
                          (name, protocol, ts))
        self.conn.commit()
        return self.get_profile_id(name)

    def delete_profile(self, name):
        pid = self.get_profile_id(name)
        if pid:
            self.conn.execute("DELETE FROM remote_buttons WHERE profile_id=?", (pid,))
            self.conn.execute("DELETE FROM profiles WHERE id=?", (pid,))
            self.conn.commit()

    def rename_profile(self, old, new):
        self.conn.execute("UPDATE profiles SET name=? WHERE name=?", (new, old))
        self.conn.commit()

    def add_command(self, dev, ch, h, label="", notes=""):
        ts = datetime.datetime.now().isoformat(timespec="seconds")
        c = self.conn.cursor()
        c.execute(
            "INSERT INTO commands (ts,device_address,char_uuid,hex,label,notes) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?)",
            (ts, dev, ch, h, label, notes)
        )
        self.conn.commit()
        return c.lastrowid

    def get_commands(self, limit=500):
        return self.conn.execute("SELECT * FROM commands ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ?", (limit,)).fetchall()

    def clear_commands(self):
        self.conn.execute("DELETE FROM commands")
        self.conn.commit()

    def add_remote_button(self, pid, label, hex_s, ch, group="Ungrouped", order=0,
                          is_macro=0, macro_frames=None, macro_delay_ms=60):
        c = self.conn.cursor()
        c.execute(
            """INSERT INTO remote_buttons
            (profile_id,label,hex,char_uuid,group_name,sort_order,is_macro,macro_frames,macro_delay_ms)
            VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)""",
            (pid, label, hex_s, ch, group, order, is_macro, macro_frames, macro_delay_ms)
        )
        self.conn.commit()
        return c.lastrowid

    def get_remote_buttons(self, pid):
        return self.conn.execute("""SELECT * FROM remote_buttons WHERE profile_id=?
            ORDER BY group_name, sort_order, id""", (pid,)).fetchall()

    def get_remote_button(self, bid):
        return self.conn.execute("SELECT * FROM remote_buttons WHERE id=?", (bid,)).fetchone()

    def delete_remote_button(self, bid):
        self.conn.execute("DELETE FROM remote_buttons WHERE id=?", (bid,))
        self.conn.commit()

    def update_remote_button(self, bid, label, hex_s, group):
        self.conn.execute("UPDATE remote_buttons SET label=?, hex=?, group_name=? WHERE id=?",
                          (label, hex_s, group, bid))
        self.conn.commit()

    def clone_remote_button(self, bid):
        r = self.get_remote_button(bid)
        if r:
            return self.add_remote_button(
                r["profile_id"], r["label"] + " (copy)",
                r["hex"], r["char_uuid"], r["group_name"], r["sort_order"] + 1,
                r["is_macro"], r["macro_frames"], r["macro_delay_ms"]
            )

    def upsert_device(self, address, name, ch):
        ts = datetime.datetime.now().isoformat(timespec="seconds")
        self.conn.execute("""INSERT INTO devices (address,name,last_char_uuid,last_seen)
            VALUES (?,?,?,?) ON CONFLICT(address) DO UPDATE SET
            name=excluded.name,last_char_uuid=excluded.last_char_uuid,last_seen=excluded.last_seen""",
            (address, name, ch, ts))
        self.conn.commit()

    def close(self):
        try:
            self.conn.close()
        except Exception:
            pass


class ConnectedDevice:
    def __init__(self, address, name, client, char_uuid):
        self.address = address
        self.name = name
        self.client = client
        self.char_uuid = char_uuid
        self.status = "connected"
        self.last_ok = None
        self.last_color = (0, 0, 0)


class ColorWheel(QWidget):
    colorPicked = Signal(int, int, int)

    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setMinimumSize(240, 240)
        self.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Expanding, QSizePolicy.Expanding)
        self._value = 1.0
        self._hue = 0.0
        self._sat = 1.0
        self._pixmap = None
        self._last_emit = 0.0
        self._build_wheel()

    def resizeEvent(self, e):
        self._pixmap = None
        self._build_wheel()
        super().resizeEvent(e)

    def _build_wheel(self):
        size = max(200, min(self.width(), self.height()))
        pix = QPixmap(size, size)
        pix.fill(Qt.transparent)
        p = QPainter(pix)
        p.setRenderHint(QPainter.Antialiasing)
        rect = QRectF(2, 2, size - 4, size - 4)
        conic = QConicalGradient(size / 2, size / 2, 0)
        for i in range(361):
            conic.setColorAt(i / 360.0, QColor.fromHsvF((i / 360.0) % 1.0, 1.0, 1.0))
        p.setBrush(QBrush(conic))
        p.setPen(Qt.NoPen)
        p.drawEllipse(rect)
        radial = QRadialGradient(size / 2, size / 2, size / 2)
        radial.setColorAt(0.0, QColor(255, 255, 255, 255))
        radial.setColorAt(1.0, QColor(255, 255, 255, 0))
        p.setBrush(QBrush(radial))
        p.drawEllipse(rect)
        p.end()
        self._pixmap = pix

    def set_value(self, v):
        self._value = v
        self._emit_current()

    def _emit_current(self):
        r, g, b = self._hsv(self._hue, self._sat, self._value)
        self.colorPicked.emit(r, g, b)

    @staticmethod
    def _hsv(h, s, v):
        r, g, b = colorsys.hsv_to_rgb(h, s, v)
        return int(r * 255), int(g * 255), int(b * 255)

    def paintEvent(self, e):
        if self._pixmap is None:
            self._build_wheel()
        p = QPainter(self)
        p.setRenderHint(QPainter.Antialiasing)
        w, h = self.width(), self.height()
        size = self._pixmap.width()
        x, y = (w - size) / 2, (h - size) / 2
        p.drawPixmap(int(x), int(y), self._pixmap)
        cx, cy = x + size / 2, y + size / 2
        r_max = size / 2 - 2
        ang = -self._hue * 2 * math.pi
        rr = self._sat * r_max
        px = cx + math.cos(ang) * rr
        py = cy + math.sin(ang) * rr
        p.setPen(QPen(QColor(255, 255, 255), 2))
        p.setBrush(Qt.NoBrush)
        p.drawEllipse(QPointF(px, py), 8, 8)

    def mousePressEvent(self, e):
        self._pick(e.position())

    def mouseMoveEvent(self, e):
        if e.buttons() & Qt.LeftButton:
            self._pick(e.position())

    def mouseReleaseEvent(self, e):
        self._last_emit = 0
        self._emit_current()

    def _pick(self, pos):
        w, h = self.width(), self.height()
        size = self._pixmap.width() if self._pixmap else min(w, h)
        cx, cy = w / 2, h / 2
        dx, dy = pos.x() - cx, pos.y() - cy
        r = math.hypot(dx, dy)
        r_max = size / 2 - 2
        if r > r_max:
            r = r_max
        self._hue = (-math.atan2(dy, dx) / (2 * math.pi)) % 1.0
        self._sat = r / r_max
        self.update()
        now = time.time()
        if now - self._last_emit < 0.06:
            return
        self._last_emit = now
        self._emit_current()


class StripView(QWidget):
    def __init__(self, rows=15, cols=10, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.rows = rows
        self.cols = cols
        self.base = QColor(0, 0, 0)
        self.setMinimumHeight(rows * 14 + 14)
        self.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Expanding, QSizePolicy.Fixed)

    def set_color(self, r, g, b):
        self.base = QColor(r, g, b)
        self.update()

    def paintEvent(self, e):
        p = QPainter(self)
        w, h = self.width(), self.height()
        cw, ch = w / self.cols, (h - 10) / self.rows
        for r in range(self.rows):
            for c in range(self.cols):
                x, y = c * cw, 10 + r * ch
                p.fillRect(QRectF(x + 1, y + 1, cw - 2, ch - 2), self.base)
        p.setPen(QPen(QColor(120, 120, 130), 1))
        p.drawLine(0, 5, w, 5)
        p.drawLine(0, h - 4, w, h - 4)


class MusicEngine:
    def __init__(self, sr=22050, block=1024):
        self.sr = sr
        self.block = block
        self.audio_q = queue.Queue(maxsize=8)
        self.stream = None
        self.file_stop = threading.Event()
        self._file_data = None
        self._file_pos = 0
        self.bass = self.mid = self.treble = self.rms = 0.0
        self.beat = False
        self._last_bass = 0.0
        self._cooldown = 0.0
        self.energy = 0.0
        self._last_tick = time.time()

    def list_inputs(self):
        if not load_audio():
            return []
        out = []
        try:
            hostapis = sd.query_hostapis()
            for i, d in enumerate(sd.query_devices()):
                if d["max_input_channels"] > 0:
                    api = hostapis[d["hostapi"]]["name"] if d.get("hostapi") is not None else "?"
                    out.append((i, d["name"], api))
        except Exception as e:
            log_error(f"list_inputs: {e}")
        return out

    def list_outputs(self):
        if not load_audio():
            return []
        out = []
        try:
            hostapis = sd.query_hostapis()
            for i, d in enumerate(sd.query_devices()):
                if d["max_output_channels"] > 0:
                    api = hostapis[d["hostapi"]]["name"] if d.get("hostapi") is not None else "?"
                    out.append((i, d["name"], api))
        except Exception as e:
            log_error(f"list_outputs: {e}")
        return out

    def start_mic(self, idx):
        if not load_audio():
            raise RuntimeError("audio unavailable")

        def cb(indata, frames, t, status):
            try:
                self.audio_q.put_nowait(indata.copy())
            except queue.Full:
                pass

        self.stream = sd.InputStream(
            device=idx,
            channels=1,
            samplerate=self.sr,
            blocksize=self.block,
            callback=cb
        )
        self.stream.start()

    def start_system_loopback(self, output_index=None):
        if not load_audio():
            raise RuntimeError("audio unavailable")
        if sys.platform != "win32":
            raise RuntimeError("System loopback only on Windows here.")

        def _cb(indata, frames, t, status):
            try:
                self.audio_q.put_nowait(indata.copy())
            except queue.Full:
                pass

        out_dev = output_index if output_index is not None else sd.default.device[1]
        info = sd.query_devices(out_dev)

        try:
            settings = sd.WasapiSettings(loopback=True)
            self.stream = sd.InputStream(
                device=out_dev,
                channels=min(2, info["max_output_channels"]),
                samplerate=self.sr,
                blocksize=self.block,
                extra_settings=settings,
                callback=_cb
            )
            self.stream.start()
            emit("info", f"Loopback via WASAPI on {info['name']}")
            return
        except TypeError:
            emit("error", "WASAPI loopback not supported in this sounddevice build")
        except Exception as e:
            emit("error", f"WASAPI loopback failed: {e}")

        for i, d in enumerate(sd.query_devices()):
            name = d["name"].lower()
            if d["max_input_channels"] > 0 and ("loopback" in name or "stereo mix" in name or "what u hear" in name):
                self.stream = sd.InputStream(
                    device=i,
                    channels=min(2, d["max_input_channels"]),
                    samplerate=self.sr,
                    blocksize=self.block,
                    callback=_cb
                )
                self.stream.start()
                emit("info", f"Loopback via input device: {d['name']}")
                return
        raise RuntimeError("No WASAPI loopback available. Use 'Microphone' mode and pick VoiceMeeter Output directly.")

    def start_file(self, path):
        if not load_audio():
            raise RuntimeError("audio unavailable")
        import soundfile as sf
        self.file_stop.clear()
        data, sr = sf.read(path, dtype="float32", always_2d=True)
        if data.shape[1] > 1:
            data = data.mean(axis=1, keepdims=True)
        self._file_data = data
        self._file_pos = 0

        def cb(outdata, frames, t, status):
            if self.file_stop.is_set():
                outdata[:] = 0
                raise sd.CallbackStop
            rem = len(self._file_data) - self._file_pos
            if rem <= 0:
                outdata[:] = 0
                raise sd.CallbackStop
            take = min(frames, rem)
            outdata[:take] = self._file_data[self._file_pos:self._file_pos + take]
            outdata[take:] = 0
            self._file_pos += take
            try:
                self.audio_q.put_nowait(outdata[:take].copy())
            except queue.Full:
                pass

        self.stream = sd.OutputStream(samplerate=sr, channels=1, callback=cb)
        self.stream.start()

    def stop(self):
        self.file_stop.set()
        if self.stream:
            try:
                self.stream.stop()
                self.stream.close()
            except Exception:
                pass
            self.stream = None

    def analyse(self):
        if not load_audio():
            return
        latest = None
        while True:
            try:
                latest = self.audio_q.get_nowait()
            except queue.Empty:
                break
        if latest is None:
            return
        s = latest[:, 0].astype(np.float32)
        if len(s) < 64:
            return
        w = s * np.hanning(len(s))
        fft = np.abs(np.fft.rfft(w))
        freqs = np.fft.rfftfreq(len(s), 1.0 / self.sr)
        def band(lo, hi):
            m = (freqs >= lo) & (freqs < hi)
            return float(np.mean(fft[m])) if np.any(m) else 0.0

        self.bass = band(20, 250)
        self.mid = band(250, 2000)
        self.treble = band(2000, 8000)
        self.rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(s ** 2)))
        now = time.time()
        dt = now - self._last_tick
        self._last_tick = now
        self.energy = (self.energy + self.rms * dt) % 1.0
        self.beat = (self.bass > self._last_bass * 1.6 and self.bass > 0.02 and now > self._cooldown)
        if self.beat:
            self._cooldown = now + 0.15
        self._last_bass = 0.85 * self._last_bass + 0.15 * self.bass


class MainWindow(QMainWindow):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self.setWindowTitle(f"{APP_NAME}  {APP_VERSION}")
        self.resize(1400, 920)
        self.db = DB()
        self.connected_devices = {}
        self.scan_results = []
        self.music = MusicEngine()
        self._music_active = False
        self._closing = False
        self.send_delay_ms = 120

        self.tabs = QTabWidget()
        self.setCentralWidget(self.tabs)
        self.tabs.addTab(self._build_connect_tab(), "Connect")
        self.tabs.addTab(self._build_remote_tab(), "Remote")
        self.tabs.addTab(self._build_music_tab(), "Music Sync")
        self.tabs.addTab(self._build_lab_tab(), "Lab")
        self.tabs.addTab(self._build_console_tab(), "Console")
        self.tabs.addTab(self._build_layout_tab(), "Visual Layout")
        self.tabs.addTab(self._build_eventlog_tab(), "Event Log")

        mb = self.menuBar()
        vm = mb.addMenu("View")
        self.dark_action = QAction("Dark mode", self, checkable=True)
        self.dark_action.setChecked(self._windows_dark())
        self.dark_action.toggled.connect(self._apply_theme)
        vm.addAction(self.dark_action)
        self._apply_theme(self.dark_action.isChecked())

        self.refresh_log()
        self.refresh_profile_combo()
        self.refresh_remote()

        self.music_timer = QTimer()
        self.music_timer.setInterval(80)
        self.music_timer.timeout.connect(self._music_tick)

        self.heartbeat_timer = QTimer()
        self.heartbeat_timer.setInterval(4000)
        self.heartbeat_timer.timeout.connect(self._heartbeat)
        self.heartbeat_timer.start()

        self.event_timer = QTimer()
        self.event_timer.setInterval(250)
        self.event_timer.timeout.connect(self._drain_events)
        self.event_timer.start()

        emit("info", f"{APP_NAME} {APP_VERSION} started")
        self.statusBar().showMessage("Ready — MR Star v1.3")

    def _windows_dark(self):
        try:
            import winreg
            k = winreg.OpenKey(
                winreg.HKEY_CURRENT_USER,
                r"Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Themes\Personalize"
            )
            v, _ = winreg.QueryValueEx(k, "AppsUseLightTheme")
            return v == 0
        except Exception:
            return False

    def _apply_theme(self, dark):
        if dark:
            self.setStyleSheet("""
                QWidget { background:#1c1c1e; color:#f2f2f7; }
                QTabWidget::pane { border:1px solid #2c2c2e; background:#1c1c1e; }
                QTabBar::tab { background:#2c2c2e; color:#f2f2f7; padding:6px 14px; }
                QTabBar::tab:selected { background:#3a3a3c; }
                QPushButton { background:#2c2c2e; border:1px solid #3a3a3c;
                    padding:6px 10px; border-radius:6px; }
                QPushButton:hover { background:#3a3a3c; }
                QLineEdit, QComboBox, QSpinBox, QTextEdit, QPlainTextEdit, QListWidget,
                QTableWidget { background:#2c2c2e; color:#f2f2f7;
                    border:1px solid #3a3a3c; border-radius:4px; }
                QGroupBox { border:1px solid #3a3a3c; border-radius:6px; margin-top:10px; }
                QGroupBox::title { subcontrol-origin: margin; left:10px; padding:0 4px; }
                QHeaderView::section { background:#2c2c2e; color:#f2f2f7; padding:4px; }
            """)
        else:
            self.setStyleSheet("")

    def _heartbeat(self):
        changed = False
        for dev in list(self.connected_devices.values()):
            try:
                still = getattr(dev.client, "is_connected", True)
            except Exception:
                still = False
            new = "connected" if still else "disconnected"
            if new != dev.status:
                dev.status = new
                changed = True
                emit("ble" if new == "connected" else "error", f"{dev.name} → {new}")
        if changed:
            self._refresh_connected_list()
            self._refresh_layout()
            if self._music_active and not any(d.status == "connected" for d in self.connected_devices.values()):
                self._stop_music()

    # CONNECT TAB
    def _build_connect_tab(self):
        w = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout(w)
        top = QHBoxLayout()
        for txt, slot in [
            ("Scan", self._scan),
            ("Connect Checked", self._connect_checked),
            ("Disconnect All", self._disconnect_all),
            ("Forget Disconnected", self._forget_disconnected)
        ]:
            b = QPushButton(txt)
            b.clicked.connect(slot)
            top.addWidget(b)
        layout.addLayout(top)

        layout.addWidget(QLabel("Scan results (check, then Connect Checked):"))
        self.device_list = QListWidget()
        layout.addWidget(self.device_list)

        layout.addWidget(QLabel("Connected (double-click a disconnected one to reconnect):"))
        self.connected_list = QListWidget()
        self.connected_list.itemDoubleClicked.connect(self._reconnect_clicked)
        layout.addWidget(self.connected_list)
        return w

    def _scan(self):
        asyncio.create_task(self.scan_ble())

    async def scan_ble(self):
        emit("info", "BLE scan started (6s)")
        try:
            self.scan_results = await BleakScanner.discover(timeout=6.0)
            self.device_list.clear()
            for d in self.scan_results:
                it = QListWidgetItem(f"{d.name or 'Unknown'}  |  {d.address}")
                it.setFlags(it.flags() | Qt.ItemIsUserCheckable)
                it.setCheckState(Qt.Unchecked)
                it.setData(Qt.UserRole, d.address)
                it.setData(Qt.UserRole + 1, d.name or "Unknown")
                self.device_list.addItem(it)
            emit("info", f"Scan found {len(self.scan_results)} devices")
        except Exception as e:
            emit("error", f"Scan: {e}")

    def _connect_checked(self):
        asyncio.create_task(self.connect_checked())

    async def _connect_one(self, address, name):
        try:
            emit("info", f"Connecting {name} ({address})...")
            client = BleakClient(address, timeout=15.0)
            await client.connect()
            candidates = []
            for svc in client.services:
                for ch in svc.characteristics:
                    if "write" in ch.properties or "write-without-response" in ch.properties:
                        candidates.append(ch.uuid)
            char_uuid = None
            for suf in ["fff3", "ffe1", "fff2"]:
                for c in candidates:
                    if suf in c.lower():
                        char_uuid = c
                        break
                if char_uuid:
                    break
            if not char_uuid and candidates:
                char_uuid = candidates[0]
            if not char_uuid:
                await client.disconnect()
                return False

            self.connected_devices[address] = ConnectedDevice(address, name, client, char_uuid)
            self.db.upsert_device(address, name, char_uuid)
            emit("ble", f"Connected {name} | {char_uuid}")
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            emit("error", f"connect {name}: {e}")
            return False

    async def connect_checked(self):
        checked = []
        for i in range(self.device_list.count()):
            it = self.device_list.item(i)
            if it.checkState() == Qt.Checked:
                checked.append((it.data(Qt.UserRole), it.data(Qt.UserRole + 1)))
        if not checked:
            QMessageBox.information(self, "Connect", "Check at least one.")
            return
        for address, name in checked:
            if address in self.connected_devices:
                continue
            await self._connect_one(address, name)
        self._refresh_connected_list()
        self._refresh_layout()
        self.statusBar().showMessage(f"Connected to {len(self.connected_devices)}")

    async def _reconnect_addr(self, addr):
        name = None
        for d in self.scan_results:
            if d.address == addr:
                name = d.name or "Unknown"
                break
        if name is None:
            name = addr
        emit("info", f"Reconnecting {addr}...")
        await self._connect_one(addr, name)
        self._refresh_connected_list()
        self._refresh_layout()

    def _reconnect_clicked(self, item):
        try:
            parts = item.text().split("|")
            addr = parts[1].strip()
            if addr in self.connected_devices and self.connected_devices[addr].status == "connected":
                return
            asyncio.create_task(self._reconnect_addr(addr))
        except Exception as e:
            emit("error", f"reconnect: {e}")

    def _disconnect_all(self):
        asyncio.create_task(self.disconnect_all())

    async def disconnect_all(self):
        for dev in list(self.connected_devices.values()):
            try:
                await dev.client.disconnect()
                emit("ble", f"Disconnected {dev.name}")
            except Exception:
                pass
        self.connected_devices.clear()
        self._refresh_connected_list()
        self._refresh_layout()
        if self._music_active:
            self._stop_music()
        self.statusBar().showMessage("Disconnected")

    def _forget_disconnected(self):
        for a in [a for a, d in self.connected_devices.items() if d.status != "connected"]:
            emit("info", f"Forgetting {a}")
            del self.connected_devices[a]
        self._refresh_connected_list()
        self._refresh_layout()

    def _refresh_connected_list(self):
        self.connected_list.clear()
        for dev in self.connected_devices.values():
            tag = {"connected": "●", "disconnected": "○"}.get(dev.status, "?")
            self.connected_list.addItem(f"{tag}  {dev.name}  |  {dev.address}  |  {dev.status}")

    # SEND
    async def send_hex(self, hex_s, char_uuid=None, response=False, address=None):
        if not self.connected_devices:
            emit("error", "Send: no devices connected")
            return False

        clean = re.sub(r"[^0-9A-Fa-f]", "", hex_s)
        if len(clean) % 2:
            emit("error", f"Bad hex: {hex_s}")
            return False

        data = bytes.fromhex(clean)
        targets = [self.connected_devices[address]] if address in self.connected_devices else [
            d for d in self.connected_devices.values() if d.status == "connected"
        ]

        if not targets:
            return False

        results = []
        for i, dev in enumerate(targets):
            if not getattr(dev.client, "is_connected", True):
                dev.status = "disconnected"
                results.append(False)
                continue
            try:
                await dev.client.write_gatt_char(char_uuid or dev.char_uuid, data, response=response)
                dev.last_ok = True
                results.append(True)
                emit("send", f"→ {dev.name}: {hex_s}")
            except Exception:
                try:
                    await dev.client.write_gatt_char(char_uuid or dev.char_uuid, data, response=not response)
                    dev.last_ok = True
                    results.append(True)
                    emit("send", f"→ {dev.name}: {hex_s} (retry)")
                except Exception as e2:
                    dev.last_ok = False
                    if dev.status != "disconnected":
                        dev.status = "disconnected"
                        emit("error", f"DROPPED {dev.name}: {e2}")
                    results.append(False)
            if i < len(targets) - 1:
                await asyncio.sleep(self.send_delay_ms / 1000.0)
        return any(results)

    # REMOTE
    def _build_remote_tab(self):
        w = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout(w)
        top = QHBoxLayout()
        top.addWidget(QLabel("Profile:"))
        self.profile_combo = QComboBox()
        self.profile_combo.currentIndexChanged.connect(self.refresh_remote)
        top.addWidget(self.profile_combo)
        for txt, slot in [("New", self.new_profile), ("Rename", self.rename_profile), ("Delete", self.del_profile)]:
            b = QPushButton(txt)
            b.clicked.connect(slot)
            top.addWidget(b)
        top.addWidget(QLabel("Inter-device delay:"))
        self.delay_spin = QSpinBox()
        self.delay_spin.setRange(40, 500)
        self.delay_spin.setValue(self.send_delay_ms)
        self.delay_spin.setSuffix(" ms")
        self.delay_spin.valueChanged.connect(lambda v: setattr(self, "send_delay_ms", v))
        top.addWidget(self.delay_spin)
        layout.addLayout(top)

        body = QHBoxLayout()
        left = QVBoxLayout()
        right = QVBoxLayout()
        scroll = QScrollArea()
        scroll.setWidgetResizable(True)
        self.remote_container = QWidget()
        self.remote_grid = QVBoxLayout(self.remote_container)
        self.remote_grid.setAlignment(Qt.AlignTop)
        scroll.setWidget(self.remote_container)
        left.addWidget(scroll)

        wheel_box = QGroupBox("Color")
        wl = QVBoxLayout(wheel_box)
        self.color_wheel = ColorWheel()
        self.color_wheel.colorPicked.connect(self._on_color_picked)
        wl.addWidget(self.color_wheel)

        brow = QHBoxLayout()
        brow.addWidget(QLabel(f"Brightness ({BRIGHTNESS_MIN}-{BRIGHTNESS_MAX}):"))
        self.bright_slider = QSlider(Qt.Horizontal)
        self.bright_slider.setRange(BRIGHTNESS_MIN, BRIGHTNESS_MAX)
        self.bright_slider.setValue(BRIGHTNESS_MAX)
        self.bright_slider.valueChanged.connect(self._on_brightness)
        brow.addWidget(self.bright_slider)
        wl.addLayout(brow)

        self.pure_check = QCheckBox("Full saturation (uncheck for soft/pale)")
        self.pure_check.setChecked(True)
        wl.addWidget(self.pure_check)

        self.color_swatch = QLabel()
        self.color_swatch.setFixedHeight(28)
        self.color_swatch.setStyleSheet("background:#ff0000; border-radius:4px;")
        wl.addWidget(self.color_swatch)

        self.strip_view = StripView(15, 10)
        self.strip_view.set_color(0, 0, 0)
        wl.addWidget(QLabel("Strip preview:"))
        wl.addWidget(self.strip_view)
        right.addWidget(wheel_box)

        body.addLayout(left, 3)
        body.addLayout(right, 2)
        layout.addLayout(body, 1)
        return w

    def _on_brightness(self):
        self.color_wheel.set_value(self.bright_slider.value() / BRIGHTNESS_MAX)

    def _on_color_picked(self, r, g, b):
        self.color_swatch.setStyleSheet(f"background:rgb({r},{g},{b}); border-radius:4px;")
        self.strip_view.set_color(r, g, b)

        for dev in self.connected_devices.values():
            if dev.status == "connected":
                dev.last_color = (r, g, b)

        bri = self.bright_slider.value()
        pure = self.pure_check.isChecked()
        color_hex = encode_mrstar_color(r, g, b, brightness=(bri / BRIGHTNESS_MAX), pure=pure)
        brightness_hex = encode_mrstar_brightness(bri / BRIGHTNESS_MAX)
        asyncio.create_task(self.send_hex(color_hex))
        asyncio.create_task(self.send_hex(brightness_hex))

    def refresh_profile_combo(self):
        self.profile_combo.blockSignals(True)
        cur = self.profile_combo.currentText()
        self.profile_combo.clear()
        for p in self.db.get_profiles():
            self.profile_combo.addItem(p["name"])
        idx = self.profile_combo.findText(cur)
        if idx >= 0:
            self.profile_combo.setCurrentIndex(idx)
        self.profile_combo.blockSignals(False)
        self.refresh_remote()

    def new_profile(self):
        n, ok = QInputDialog.getText(self, "New profile", "Name:")
        if ok and n:
            try:
                self.db.create_profile(n)
                self.refresh_profile_combo()
            except sqlite3.IntegrityError:
                QMessageBox.warning(self, "Exists", "Exists.")

    def del_profile(self):
        n = self.profile_combo.currentText()
        if n and QMessageBox.question(self, "Delete", f"Delete '{n}'?") == QMessageBox.Yes:
            self.db.delete_profile(n)
            self.refresh_profile_combo()
            self.refresh_remote()

    def rename_profile(self):
        old = self.profile_combo.currentText()
        new, ok = QInputDialog.getText(self, "Rename", "New:", text=old)
        if ok and new and new != old:
            self.db.rename_profile(old, new)
            self.refresh_profile_combo()

    def refresh_remote(self):
        while self.remote_grid.count():
            it = self.remote_grid.takeAt(0)
            wdg = it.widget()
            if wdg:
                wdg.deleteLater()
        pid = self.db.get_profile_id(self.profile_combo.currentText())
        if not pid:
            return

        rows = self.db.get_remote_buttons(pid)
        groups = {}
        for r in rows:
            groups.setdefault(r["group_name"], []).append(r)

        for gname, items in groups.items():
            box = QGroupBox(gname)
            grid = QGridLayout(box)
            for i, r in enumerate(items):
                wrap = QWidget()
                wl = QHBoxLayout(wrap)
                wl.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
                btn = QPushButton(r["label"] + ("  [macro]" if r["is_macro"] else ""))
                btn.setToolTip(f"Hex: {r['hex']}")
                if r["is_macro"]:
                    frames = json.loads(r["macro_frames"] or "[]")
                    d = r["macro_delay_ms"]
                    btn.clicked.connect(lambda _, f=frames, dd=d: asyncio.create_task(self._play_macro(f, dd)))
                else:
                    btn.clicked.connect(lambda _, h=r["hex"]: asyncio.create_task(self.send_hex(h)))
                btn.setContextMenuPolicy(Qt.CustomContextMenu)
                btn.customContextMenuRequested.connect(lambda pos, rid=r["id"], b=btn: self._button_menu(pos, rid, b))
                wl.addWidget(btn)
                grid.addWidget(wrap, i // 3, i % 3)
            self.remote_grid.addWidget(box)

    def _button_menu(self, pos, bid, btn):
        m = QMenu(self)
        m.addAction("Edit", lambda: self._edit_button(bid))
        m.addAction("Clone", lambda: (self.db.clone_remote_button(bid), self.refresh_remote()))
        m.addAction("Delete", lambda: (self.db.delete_remote_button(bid), self.refresh_remote()))
        m.exec(btn.mapToGlobal(pos))

    def _edit_button(self, bid):
        row = self.db.get_remote_button(bid)
        if not row:
            return
        nl, ok = QInputDialog.getText(self, "Edit", "Label:", text=row["label"])
        if not ok:
            return
        nh, ok = QInputDialog.getText(self, "Edit", "Hex:", text=row["hex"])
        if not ok:
            return
        ng, ok = QInputDialog.getText(self, "Edit", "Group:", text=row["group_name"])
        if not ok:
            return
        self.db.update_remote_button(bid, nl, nh, ng)
        self.refresh_remote()

    # MUSIC
    def _build_music_tab(self):
        w = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout(w)
        src_box = QGroupBox("Source")
        sl = QVBoxLayout(src_box)

        self.file_row = QWidget()
        fl = QVBoxLayout(self.file_row)
        fl.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)

        next_btn.clicked.connect(self._next_file)

        pl = QGridLayout(pat_box)

        self.pattern_combo.addItems([
            "Rainbow wave (audio)", "Pulse brightness", "Strobe on beat",
            "Beat flash colour", "Pink pulse", "Spectrum bands"
        ])
        pl.addWidget(self.pattern_combo, 0, 1)
        pl.addWidget(QLabel("Sensitivity:"), 1, 0)
        self.sens = QSlider(Qt.Horizontal)
        self.sens.setRange(1, 40)
        self.sens.setValue(12)
        pl.addWidget(self.sens, 1, 1)
        pl.addWidget(QLabel("Speed:"), 2, 0)
        self.speed = QSlider(Qt.Horizontal)
        self.speed.setRange(1, 40)
        self.speed.setValue(15)
        pl.addWidget(self.speed, 2, 1)
        pl.addWidget(QLabel(f"Brightness cap ({BRIGHTNESS_MIN}-{BRIGHTNESS_MAX}):"), 3, 0)
        self.bright = QSlider(Qt.Horizontal)
        self.bright.setRange(BRIGHTNESS_MIN, BRIGHTNESS_MAX)
        self.bright.setValue(BRIGHTNESS_MAX)
        pl.addWidget(self.bright, 3, 1)
        layout.addWidget(pat_box)

        ctrl = QHBoxLayout()

        self.start_music_btn.clicked.connect(self._start_music)

        self.stop_music_btn.clicked.connect(self._stop_music)
        self.stop_music_btn.setEnabled(False)

        ctrl.addWidget(self.stop_music_btn)

    def _source_changed(self, idx):
        self.mic_row.setVisible(idx in (0, 1))
        self.out_row.setVisible(idx == 1)
        self.file_row.setVisible(idx == 2)

    def _populate_mics(self):
        self.mic_combo.clear()
        if not load_audio():
            return
        try:
            seen = set()
            for i, name, api in self.music.list_inputs():
                key = (name, api)
                if key in seen:
                    continue
                seen.add(key)
                self.mic_combo.addItem(f"[{api}] {name}  (idx {i})", i)
        except Exception as e:
            emit("error", f"populate mics: {e}")

    def _populate_outputs(self):
        self.out_combo.clear()
        if not load_audio():
            return
        try:
            seen = set()
            for i, name, api in self.music.list_outputs():
                key = (name, api)
                if key in seen:
                    continue
                seen.add(key)
                self.out_combo.addItem(f"[{api}] {name}  (idx {i})", i)
        except Exception as e:
            emit("error", f"populate outputs: {e}")

    def _load_files(self):
        files, _ = QFileDialog.getOpenFileNames(self, "Audio", "", "Audio (*.wav *.flac *.ogg *.mp3)")
        if files:
            self._playlist = files
            self._playlist_idx = 0
            self.playlist_widget.clear()
            for f in files:
                self.playlist_widget.addItem(os.path.basename(f))
            self.playlist_widget.setCurrentRow(0)
            self.file_label.setText(f"{len(files)} file(s) loaded")

    def _playlist_play(self, item):
        self._playlist_idx = self.playlist_widget.row(item)
        self.file_label.setText(f"Now: {os.path.basename(self._playlist[self._playlist_idx])}")
        if self._music_active:
            self.music.stop()
            self._start_file()

    def _next_file(self):
        if not self._playlist:
            return
        import random
        if self.shuffle_check.isChecked():
            self._playlist_idx = random.randrange(len(self._playlist))
        else:
            self._playlist_idx = (self._playlist_idx + 1) % len(self._playlist)
        self.playlist_widget.setCurrentRow(self._playlist_idx)
        self.file_label.setText(f"Now: {os.path.basename(self._playlist[self._playlist_idx])}")
        if self._music_active:
            self.music.stop()
            self._start_file()

    def _start_file(self):
        if not self._playlist:
            return
        path = self._playlist[self._playlist_idx]
        try:
            self.music.start_file(path)
            emit("info", f"Playing {os.path.basename(path)}")
        except Exception as e:
            emit("error", f"start_file: {e}")

    def _start_music(self):
        if not load_audio():
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Audio", "sounddevice/numpy missing")
            return
        idx = self.source_combo.currentIndex()
        try:
            if idx == 0:
                dev = self.mic_combo.currentData()
                if dev is None:
                    QMessageBox.warning(self, "Mic", "No device.")
                    return
                self.music.start_mic(dev)
            elif idx == 1:
                out_dev = self.out_combo.currentData()
                self.music.start_system_loopback(out_dev)
            elif idx == 2:
                self._start_file()
            self._music_active = True
            self.start_music_btn.setEnabled(False)
            self.stop_music_btn.setEnabled(True)
            self.music_timer.start()
            self.music_status.setText("Running")
            emit("info", "Music sync started")
        except Exception as e:
            emit("error", f"start_music: {e}")
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "Error", str(e))

    def _stop_music(self):
        self._music_active = False
        try:
            self.music.stop()
        except Exception:
            pass
        self.music_timer.stop()
        self.start_music_btn.setEnabled(True)
        self.stop_music_btn.setEnabled(False)
        self.music_status.setText("Idle")
        emit("info", "Music sync stopped")

    def _music_tick(self):
        if not self._music_active:
            return
        if not any(d.status == "connected" for d in self.connected_devices.values()):
            self._stop_music()
            self.music_status.setText("Stopped (no device)")
            return

        try:
            self.music.analyse()
        except Exception as e:
            emit("error", f"analyse: {e}")
            return

        pat = self.pattern_combo.currentText()
        sens = self.sens.value() / 10.0
        speed = self.speed.value() / 10.0
        cap = self.bright.value()
        r = g = b = 0

        if pat.startswith("Rainbow wave"):
            hue = (self.music.energy * speed * 2.0) % 1.0
            r, g, b = ColorWheel._hsv(hue, 1, 1)
        elif pat == "Pulse brightness":
            v = int(min(cap, self.music.rms * 2000 * sens))
            hex_s = encode_mrstar_color(255, 255, 255, brightness=min(BRIGHTNESS_MAX, max(BRIGHTNESS_MIN, v)) / BRIGHTNESS_MAX)
            asyncio.create_task(self.send_hex(hex_s))
            return
        elif pat == "Strobe on beat":
            v = cap if self.music.beat else 0
            hex_s = encode_mrstar_color(255, 255, 255, brightness=max(BRIGHTNESS_MIN, v) / BRIGHTNESS_MAX)
            asyncio.create_task(self.send_hex(hex_s))
            return
        elif pat == "Beat flash colour":
            if self.music.beat:
                hue = (self.music.energy * speed * 3.0) % 1.0
                r, g, b = ColorWheel._hsv(hue, 1, 1)
        elif pat == "Pink pulse":
            base = self.music.rms * sens
            r = int(min(255, 180 + base * 75))
            g = 0
            b = int(min(255, 100 + base * 100))
        elif pat == "Spectrum bands":
            r = int(min(255, self.music.bass * 255 * sens))
            g = int(min(255, self.music.mid * 255 * sens))
            b = int(min(255, self.music.treble * 255 * sens))

        scale = cap / BRIGHTNESS_MAX
        r = max(0, min(255, int(r * scale)))
        g = max(0, min(255, int(g * scale)))
        b = max(0, min(255, int(b * scale)))

        color_hex = encode_mrstar_color(r, g, b, brightness=1.0)
        brightness_hex = encode_mrstar_brightness(1.0)
        asyncio.create_task(self.send_hex(color_hex))
        asyncio.create_task(self.send_hex(brightness_hex))
        for dev in self.connected_devices.values():
            if dev.status == "connected":
                dev.last_color = (r, g, b)
        self.strip_view.set_color(r, g, b)
        self.music_status.setText(f"Running | RGB {r},{g},{b}")

    # LAB
    def _build_lab_tab(self):
        w = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout(w)
        top = QHBoxLayout()
        self.hex_input = QLineEdit()
        self.hex_input.setPlaceholderText("BC0406000003E8000055")

        layout.addWidget(test_box)

        macro_box = QGroupBox("Macro / Scroll")
        ml = QVBoxLayout(macro_box)
        self.macro_text = QTextEdit()
        ml.addWidget(self.macro_text)
        mrow = QHBoxLayout()
        self.macro_delay = QSpinBox()
        self.macro_delay.setRange(10, 2000)
        self.macro_delay.setValue(55)
        self.macro_delay.setSuffix(" ms")
        for txt, slot in [("Load Scroll", self._load_scroll),
                          ("Save as Button", self._save_macro),
                          ("Play", lambda: asyncio.create_task(self._play_macro_from_text()))]:
            b = QPushButton(txt)
            b.clicked.connect(slot)
            mrow.addWidget(b)
        mrow.insertWidget(0, self.macro_delay)
        ml.addLayout(mrow)
        layout.addWidget(macro_box)
        return w

    def _send_rgb_test(self):
        r, g, b = self.qr.value(), self.qg.value(), self.qb.value()
        bri = self.qbri.value()
        hex_s = encode_mrstar_color(r, g, b, brightness=(bri / BRIGHTNESS_MAX))
        brightness_hex = encode_mrstar_brightness(bri / BRIGHTNESS_MAX)
        self.hex_input.setText(hex_s)
        self.label_input.setText(f"RGB {r},{g},{b} @ {bri}")
        asyncio.create_task(self.send_hex(hex_s))
        asyncio.create_task(self.send_hex(brightness_hex))

    def _send_and_log(self):
        asyncio.create_task(self.send_and_log())

    async def send_and_log(self):
        h = self.hex_input.text().strip()
        l = self.label_input.text().strip()
        if not h:
            return
        ok = await self.send_hex(h, response=self.response_check.isChecked())
        if ok:
            for dev in self.connected_devices.values():
                if dev.status == "connected":
                    self.db.add_command(dev.address, dev.char_uuid, h, l)
            self.refresh_log()

    def refresh_log(self):
        rows = self.db.get_commands()
        self.log_table.setRowCount(len(rows))
        for i, r in enumerate(rows):
            for j, k in enumerate(["id", "ts", "device_address", "char_uuid", "hex", "label"]):
                self.log_table.setItem(i, j, QTableWidgetItem(str(r[k] or "")))
        self.log_table.resizeColumnsToContents()

    def clear_log(self):
        if QMessageBox.question(self, "Clear", "Delete all?") == QMessageBox.Yes:
            self.db.clear_commands()
            self.refresh_log()

    def diff_selected(self):
        sel = self.log_table.selectionModel().selectedRows()
        if len(sel) != 2:
            return
        rows = [
            (self.log_table.item(i.row(), 5).text(),
             self.log_table.item(i.row(), 4).text())
            for i in sel
        ]
        a = bytes.fromhex(re.sub(r"[^0-9A-Fa-f]", "", rows[0][1]))
        b = bytes.fromhex(re.sub(r"[^0-9A-Fa-f]", "", rows[1][1]))
        lines = [f"{rows[0][0]} vs {rows[1][0]}", ""]
        for i in range(max(len(a), len(b))):
            av = f"{a[i]:02X}" if i < len(a) else "--"
            bv = f"{b[i]:02X}" if i < len(b) else "--"
            lines.append(f"{i:03d}: {av}  {bv}{'  ' if av == bv else ' *'}")
        QMessageBox.information(self, "Diff", "\n".join(lines))

    def add_selected_to_remote(self):
        sel = self.log_table.selectionModel().selectedRows()
        if not sel:
            return
        pid = self.db.get_profile_id(self.profile_combo.currentText())
        group, ok = QInputDialog.getText(self, "Group", "Group:", text="Colors")
        if not ok:
            group = "Ungrouped"
        for i in sel:
            r = i.row()
            self.db.add_remote_button(pid, self.log_table.item(r, 5).text() or "Btn",
                                      self.log_table.item(r, 4).text(),
                                      self.log_table.item(r, 3).text(), group)
        self.refresh_remote()

    def _load_scroll(self):
        self.macro_text.setPlainText("\n".join(SCROLL_MACRO))

    def _save_macro(self):
        frames = [x.strip() for x in self.macro_text.toPlainText().splitlines() if x.strip()]
        if not frames:
            return
        label, ok = QInputDialog.getText(self, "Name", "Name:", text="Macro")
        if not ok or not label:
            return
        pid = self.db.get_profile_id(self.profile_combo.currentText())
        self.db.add_remote_button(pid, label, frames[0], None, "Macros",
                                  is_macro=1, macro_frames=json.dumps(frames),
                                  macro_delay_ms=self.macro_delay.value())
        self.refresh_remote()

    async def _play_macro_from_text(self):
        frames = [x.strip() for x in self.macro_text.toPlainText().splitlines() if x.strip()]
        await self._play_macro(frames, self.macro_delay.value())

    async def _play_macro(self, frames, delay_ms):
        emit("info", f"Macro start ({len(frames)} frames @ {delay_ms}ms)")
        for f in frames:
            if not self.connected_devices or self._closing:
                break
            if not any(d.status == "connected" for d in self.connected_devices.values()):
                break
            await self.send_hex(f, response=False)
            await asyncio.sleep(delay_ms / 1000.0)
        emit("info", "Macro done")

    # CONSOLE
    def _build_console_tab(self):
        w = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout(w)
        layout.addWidget(QLabel(
            "Batch runner — one command per line. Optional per-line delay: "
            "`HEX | 200` (200 ms). Comments start with #."
        ))
        top = QHBoxLayout()
        top.addWidget(QLabel("Default delay:"))
        self.con_default = QSpinBox()
        self.con_default.setRange(10, 5000)
        self.con_default.setValue(120)
        self.con_default.setSuffix(" ms")
        top.addWidget(self.con_default)
        top.addWidget(QLabel("Inter-device:"))
        self.con_dev = QSpinBox()
        self.con_dev.setRange(0, 500)
        self.con_dev.setValue(self.send_delay_ms)
        self.con_dev.setSuffix(" ms")
        self.con_dev.valueChanged.connect(lambda v: setattr(self, "send_delay_ms", v))
        top.addWidget(self.con_dev)
        top.addStretch(1)
        layout.addLayout(top)

        self.console_input = QPlainTextEdit()
        self.console_input.setPlaceholderText(
            "# comments allowed\nBC0406000003E8000055\n"
            "BC0406007803E8000055 | 300\n"
            "BC040600F003E8000055 | 300\n"
            "BC01010055 | 500"
        )
        layout.addWidget(self.console_input, 3)

        ctrl = QHBoxLayout()
        run_btn = QPushButton("▶ Run")
        run_btn.clicked.connect(self._console_run)
        stop_btn = QPushButton("■ Cancel")
        stop_btn.clicked.connect(self._console_cancel)
        test_one = QPushButton("Test 1st Line")
        test_one.clicked.connect(self._console_test_first)
        clear_btn = QPushButton("Clear")
        clear_btn.clicked.connect(self.console_input.clear)
        ctrl.addWidget(run_btn)
        ctrl.addWidget(test_one)
        ctrl.addWidget(stop_btn)
        ctrl.addWidget(clear_btn)
        ctrl.addStretch(1)
        layout.addLayout(ctrl)

        self.console_out = QPlainTextEdit()
        self.console_out.setReadOnly(True)
        layout.addWidget(QLabel("Run output:"))
        layout.addWidget(self.console_out, 2)
        self._console_cancel_flag = False
        return w

    def _console_parse(self):
        lines = []
        default_d = self.con_default.value()
        for raw in self.console_input.toPlainText().splitlines():
            line = raw.strip()
            if not line or line.startswith("#"):
                continue
            if "|" in line:
                parts = line.rsplit("|", 1)
                hex_s = parts[0].strip()
                try:
                    d = int(parts[1].strip())
                except ValueError:
                    d = default_d
            else:
                hex_s = line
                d = default_d

            clean = re.sub(r"[^0-9A-Fa-f]", "", hex_s)
            if len(clean) % 2 != 0 or not clean:
                self._console_log(f"SKIP (bad hex): {line}")
                continue
            lines.append((clean, d))
        return lines

    def _console_log(self, s):
        self.console_out.appendPlainText(s)
        emit("info", f"[Console] {s}")

    def _console_cancel(self):
        self._console_cancel_flag = True
        self._console_log("Cancel requested…")

    def _console_test_first(self):
        lines = self._console_parse()
        if not lines:
            return
        asyncio.create_task(self._console_run_batch([lines[0]]))

    def _console_run(self):
        lines = self._console_parse()
        if not lines:
            self._console_log("Nothing to run.")
            return
        self._console_cancel_flag = False
        asyncio.create_task(self._console_run_batch(lines))

    async def _console_run_batch(self, lines):
        self._console_log(f"Starting batch: {len(lines)} command(s)")
        t0 = time.time()
        for i, (hex_s, delay_ms) in enumerate(lines):
            if self._console_cancel_flag:
                self._console_log(f"Cancelled at line {i}")
                break
            ok = await self.send_hex(hex_s, response=False)
            status = "OK" if ok else "FAIL"
            self._console_log(f"  [{i+1:03d}/{len(lines):03d}] {hex_s} → {status}  (wait {delay_ms}ms)")
            for dev in self.connected_devices.values():
                if dev.status == "connected":
                    self.db.add_command(dev.address, dev.char_uuid, hex_s, f"console {i+1}")
            await asyncio.sleep(delay_ms / 1000.0)
        self.refresh_log()
        self._console_log(f"Done in {time.time()-t0:.2f}s")

    # LAYOUT
    def _build_layout_tab(self):
        w = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout(w)
        layout.addWidget(QLabel(
            "Live preview per strip. Each strip shows its last-sent colour. "
            "Note: these strips are NOT addressable — every LED on one strip is the same colour."
        ))
        self.layout_area = QVBoxLayout()
        holder = QWidget()
        holder.setLayout(self.layout_area)
        scroll = QScrollArea()
        scroll.setWidgetResizable(True)
        scroll.setWidget(holder)
        layout.addWidget(scroll)
        self.layout_views = {}
        self.layout_timer = QTimer()
        self.layout_timer.setInterval(600)
        self.layout_timer.timeout.connect(self._refresh_layout)
        self.layout_timer.start()
        return w

    def _refresh_layout(self):
        current = set(self.connected_devices.keys())
        shown = set(self.layout_views.keys())
        if current != shown:
            while self.layout_area.count():
                it = self.layout_area.takeAt(0)
                wdg = it.widget()
                if wdg:
                    wdg.deleteLater()
            self.layout_views.clear()
            for addr, dev in self.connected_devices.items():
                box = QGroupBox(f"{dev.name}  —  {addr}  [{dev.status}]")
                bl = QVBoxLayout(box)
                sv = StripView(15, 10)
                bl.addWidget(sv)
                info_row = QHBoxLayout()
                swatch = QLabel()
                swatch.setFixedSize(24, 24)
                swatch.setStyleSheet("background:#000; border:1px solid #555;")
                swatch.setProperty("swatch", True)
                info_row.addWidget(swatch)
                hex_lbl = QLabel("—")
                hex_lbl.setProperty("hex_lbl", True)
                info_row.addWidget(hex_lbl)
                info_row.addStretch(1)
                bl.addLayout(info_row)
                self.layout_area.addWidget(box)
                self.layout_views[addr] = (sv, swatch, hex_lbl)

        for addr, (sv, swatch, hex_lbl) in self.layout_views.items():
            dev = self.connected_devices.get(addr)
            if not dev:
                continue
            r, g, b = dev.last_color
            if dev.status == "disconnected":
                swatch.setStyleSheet("background:#444; border:1px solid #555;")
                hex_lbl.setText("disconnected")
                sv.set_color(40, 40, 40)
            else:
                swatch.setStyleSheet(f"background:rgb({r},{g},{b}); border:1px solid #555;")
                hex_lbl.setText(f"#{r:02X}{g:02X}{b:02X}")
                sv.set_color(r, g, b)

    # EVENT LOG
    def _build_eventlog_tab(self):
        w = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout(w)
        top = QHBoxLayout()
        top.addWidget(QLabel("Filter:"))
        self.ev_filter = QComboBox()
        self.ev_filter.addItems(["All", "info", "ble", "send", "error"])
        top.addWidget(self.ev_filter)
        self.ev_autoscroll = QCheckBox("Auto-scroll")
        self.ev_autoscroll.setChecked(True)
        top.addWidget(self.ev_autoscroll)
        clear_btn = QPushButton("Clear")
        clear_btn.clicked.connect(lambda: self.ev_log.clear())
        top.addWidget(clear_btn)
        save_btn = QPushButton("Save")
        save_btn.clicked.connect(self._save_eventlog)
        top.addWidget(save_btn)
        top.addStretch(1)
        layout.addLayout(top)

        self.ev_log = QPlainTextEdit()
        self.ev_log.setReadOnly(True)
        f = QFont("Consolas")
        f.setPointSize(9)
        self.ev_log.setFont(f)
        layout.addWidget(self.ev_log)
        return w

    def _drain_events(self):
        count = 0
        while count < 500:
            try:
                ts, kind, msg = _events.get_nowait()
            except queue.Empty:
                break
            filt = self.ev_filter.currentText()
            if filt != "All" and filt != kind:
                count += 1
                continue
            self.ev_log.appendPlainText(f"{ts}  [{kind:5s}]  {msg}")
            count += 1
        if self.ev_autoscroll.isChecked() and count:
            sb = self.ev_log.verticalScrollBar()
            sb.setValue(sb.maximum())

    def _save_eventlog(self):
        path, _ = QFileDialog.getSaveFileName(
            self, "Save event log",
            os.path.join(app_dir(), f"events_{datetime.datetime.now():%Y%m%d_%H%M%S}.txt"),
            "Text (*.txt *.log)"
        )
        if not path:
            return
        try:
            with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                f.write(self.ev_log.toPlainText())
            emit("info", f"Event log saved: {path}")
        except Exception as e:
            emit("error", f"save event log: {e}")

    # SHUTDOWN
    def closeEvent(self, event):
        self._closing = True
        for t in (self.music_timer, self.layout_timer, self.heartbeat_timer, self.event_timer):
            try:
                t.stop()
            except Exception:
                pass
        try:
            self.music.stop()
        except Exception:
            pass
        if self.connected_devices:
            try:
                loop = asyncio.get_event_loop()
                async def dc():
                    for dev in list(self.connected_devices.values()):
                        try:
                            await dev.client.disconnect()
                        except Exception:
                            pass
                fut = asyncio.ensure_future(dc())
                deadline = time.time() + 3.0
                while not fut.done() and time.time() < deadline:
                    try:
                        loop.run_until_complete(asyncio.sleep(0.05))
                    except Exception:
                        break
            except Exception:
                pass
            self.connected_devices.clear()
        try:
            self.db.close()
        except Exception:
            pass
        event.accept()


def main():
    sys.excepthook = excepthook
    app = QApplication(sys.argv)
    loop = qasync.QEventLoop(app)
    asyncio.set_event_loop(loop)
    win = MainWindow()
    icon_path = find_icon()
    if icon_path:
        icon = QIcon(icon_path)
        if not icon.isNull():
            app.setWindowIcon(icon)
            win.setWindowIcon(icon)
            emit("info", f"Icon loaded: {icon_path}")
    win.show()
    try:
        with loop:
            loop.run_forever()
    except Exception as e:
        log_error(f"crash: {e}\n{traceback.format_exc()}")
        raise

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

if (-not $py) {
    Write-Host "Python 3.12 not found on PATH."
    Write-Host "Please install Python 3.12 x64 from python.org, then rerun this script."
    exit 1
}

& py -V

Write-Host "Installing required packages..."
& py -m pip install --upgrade pip setuptools wheel
& py -m pip install -r .\requirements.txt

Write-Host "Installing Visual C++ runtime if it is missing..."

Write-Host "Done."
File: README.md

Markdown
# LumenForge

LumenForge is a Python + PySide6 desktop app for controlling BLE LED strips and bulbs that use MR Star / MR-Star-style protocol families.

## What it fixes

- Corrects the protocol mistake that mixed color and brightness into one command;
- separates MR Star color from brightness;
- keeps a captured scroll macro as experimental / exact packet replay;
- keeps Classic Magic Home as a separate legacy protocol profile;
- logs every command with a label, device, and timestamp;
- adds a remote builder and command catalog;
- keeps a music-sync mode for audio-driven lighting.

## Important protocol note

This app uses the documented MR Star format as the default path:

- Color: `BC0406HHHHSSSS000055`
- Brightness: `BC0506BBBB0000000055`
- Power: `BC01010155`

This is different from the older `BC0401RRGGBB0055` and `56RRGGBB00F0AA` patterns that were used in some testing scripts. Those are retained only as compatibility / reverse-engineering options, not as the default.

## Requirements

- Python 3.12 x64
- Windows 10/11
- Bluetooth enabled
- Optional: installed Visual C++ Redistributable 2022

## Install

```powershell
py -m pip install --upgrade pip setuptools wheel
py -m pip install -r requirements.txt
```

## Run

```powershell
python .\main.py
```

or launch with:

```bat
Launch LumenForge.bat
```

## Notes

- Use Chrome or Edge for BLE scanning if you want Web Bluetooth-like behavior in browser-based tools, but this Python app uses Bleak directly.
- The project is optimized for Python 3.12.
- For strict reverse engineering, keep the “experimental” script commands in a separate lab and never merge them into the default live UI.
If you want, the next step I can do for you is one of these:

produce a tiny “safe test script” that blasts only one strip with red/green/blue to verify the protocol in a controlled way
produce a “clean version” of the PySide6 app with only Connect + Remote + Lab tabs, stripped down for easier testing
produce a “hardware verification checklist” so you can test the strip without guessing whether the command is working
If you want, I’ll do the hardware-test version next so you can validate one strip at a time with zero confusion.

ok let's produce a tiny “safe test script” that blasts only one strip with red/green/blue to verify the protocol in a controlled way using the consol tab in the app, and make more than one test so I can tell you the resuls
if you can utilize my camera to do so it would be much better, but keep in mind I want to test all 3 light strips at the same time, not one at a time
Yes. The safest test is to send the same documented MR Star sequence to all three connected strips, while testing one color and brightness level at a time.

Because your current Console tab broadcasts to every connected device, you can use it immediately.

First: make sure all three strips are connected
Connect these three devices:

Text
41:42:59:F1:C8:68
41:42:43:E7:8B:F6
41:42:F9:D7:45:B0
Do not run any music mode during the test. Close the phone app and disable Bluetooth on the phone so it cannot compete with LumenForge.

Important protocol detail
For documented MR Star commands:

Color and brightness are separate.
Send the color command first.
Send the brightness command second.
Send BC01010155 before each test group.
Do not use the old 56... or BC0401... commands for this test.

Test 1 — Basic primary colors at full brightness
Paste this into the Console tab:

Text
# TEST 1 - RED
BC01010155 | 200
BC0406000003E8000055 | 200
BC0506040000000055 | 2500

# TEST 1 - GREEN
BC01010155 | 200
BC0406007803E8000055 | 200
BC0506040000000055 | 2500

# TEST 1 - BLUE
BC01010155 | 200
BC040600F003E8000055 | 200
BC0506040000000055 | 2500

# TEST 1 - OFF
BC01010055 | 1000
Expected documented colors:

Text
BC0406000003E8000055 = red
BC0406007803E8000055 = green
BC040600F003E8000055 = blue
Please record for each strip:

Text
Red:     correct / pale / wrong
Green:   correct / pale / wrong
Blue:    correct / pale / wrong
Also note whether all three strips behave identically.

Test 2 — Primary colors at 50% brightness
Text
# TEST 2 - RED 50%
BC01010155 | 200
BC0406000003E8000055 | 200
BC0506020000000055 | 2500

# TEST 2 - GREEN 50%
BC01010155 | 200
BC0406007803E8000055 | 200
BC0506020000000055 | 2500

# TEST 2 - BLUE 50%
BC01010155 | 200
BC040600F003E8000055 | 200
BC0506020000000055 | 2500

# TEST 2 - OFF
BC01010055 | 1000
The brightness command is:

Text
BC0506020000000055
That represents approximately 50% using the documented MR Star library’s 0–1024 brightness range.

Expected result:

Same hue as Test 1.
Approximately half the light output.
No added white.
The strip should actually become noticeably dimmer.
Test 3 — Brightness sweep using red only
This test determines whether brightness is operating independently from color.

Text
# RED 100%
BC01010155 | 200
BC0406000003E8000055 | 200
BC0506040000000055 | 1800

# RED 75%
BC0406000003E8000055 | 200
BC0506030000000055 | 1800

# RED 50%
BC0406000003E8000055 | 200
BC0506020000000055 | 1800

# RED 25%
BC0406000003E8000055 | 200
BC0506010000000055 | 1800

# RED 0%
BC0506000000000055 | 1500

# OFF
BC01010055 | 1000
Expected result:

Text
100% → brightest red
75%  → dimmer red
50%  → half-bright red
25%  → very dim red
0%   → completely dark
If 0% does not turn the strip dark, tell me exactly what happens.

Test 4 — Secondary colors
Text
# YELLOW
BC01010155 | 200
BC0406003C03E8000055 | 200
BC0506040000000055 | 1800

# CYAN
BC040600B403E8000055 | 200
BC0506040000000055 | 1800

# MAGENTA
BC0406001E03E8000055 | 200
BC0506040000000055 | 1800

# WHITE / NEUTRAL TEST
BC040600000000000055 | 200
BC0506040000000055 | 1800

# OFF
BC01010055 | 1000
Expected hues:

Text
Yellow   = approximately 60 degrees
Cyan     = approximately 180 degrees
Magenta  = approximately 300 degrees
Important: the neutral test is included only as a protocol observation. The published library supports hue/saturation color conversion, but your particular controller may handle neutral white differently.

Test 5 — Compare the three strips together
This test sends different colors to each strip individually. It is useful if you want to see whether the three controllers use different firmware.

The current Console tab broadcasts commands to all connected strips, so it cannot target different colors to individual devices. For this test, use the following separate commands only if the application has an address-selection feature. Otherwise, skip Test 5 for now.

The existing send_hex() method has an address argument internally, but the Console UI does not expose it yet.

Optional camera verification
A camera can verify the light physically, but it cannot be reliable until the camera knows where each strip is located.

The correct camera test should measure:

average RGB;
brightness;
white contamination;
difference between expected and observed color;
each strip separately.
For example:

Text
Expected: pure red
Observed: RGB(224, 36, 42)
White contamination: low
Result: PASS
However, the camera must be:

fixed in position;
pointed at all three strips;
kept under stable ambient lighting;
manually calibrated for three rectangular regions.
Your current application does not yet contain this camera calibration feature. I do not recommend adding automatic camera judgment directly into the existing Console tab until the basic BLE protocol test confirms that the commands are correct.

Better camera test design
The next version should add a Hardware Test tab with:

Select camera
Show camera preview
Define Strip 1 region
Define Strip 2 region
Define Strip 3 region
Run synchronized test
Capture each region after every command
Export results to CSV and JSON
The test result should look like:

Text
Test 03 - Red 50%

Strip 1:
  Expected RGB: 255,0,0
  Observed RGB: 211,18,23
  Brightness: 51%
  White contamination: 4%
  Result: PASS

Strip 2:
  Expected RGB: 255,0,0
  Observed RGB: 201,29,34
  Brightness: 48%
  White contamination: 7%
  Result: PASS

Strip 3:
  Expected RGB: 255,0,0
  Observed RGB: 227,41,45
  Brightness: 56%
  White contamination: 9%
  Result: PASS
One correction to the earlier generated code
Do not use this malformed white encoder from the previous response:

New safe test behavior
The new test will have these stages:

Text
1. Connect all selected strips
2. RGB connection confirmation pulse

4. Green 100% — hold 2 seconds
5. Blue 100% — hold 2 seconds

7. Red 75% — hold 2 seconds
8. Red 50% — hold 2 seconds
9. Red 25% — hold 2 seconds
10. Red 0% — hold 2 seconds
11. Restore red 100% — hold 2 seconds
12. Leave the lights ON
It will not automatically send OFF at the end.

Connection indicator
When a strip connects successfully, the app will send a very short synchronized pulse:

Text
red, 150 ms
green, 150 ms
blue, 150 ms
then restore the previous commanded state
That pulse will happen only after the connection is established. It will not be confused with a normal test result.

Because the app cannot know the physical color immediately after a BLE write, the UI will label this as:

Text

not “actual strip state confirmed.”

The write queue fix
All commands must pass through one serialized queue:

Python
async def send_sequence(self, commands, hold_seconds=2.0):
    for label, hex_command in commands:
        result = await self.send_to_all_devices(hex_command)
        self.record_test_result(label, hex_command, result)

        if self.camera_enabled:
            await asyncio.sleep(hold_seconds)
            self.camera_capture(label, hex_command, result)
        else:
            await asyncio.sleep(hold_seconds)
The important rule is:

Text
Never use create_task() for individual color and brightness commands independently.
Instead:

Python
await send_color(...)
await send_brightness(...)
await asyncio.sleep(2)
That guarantees ordering.

preserve the final static color and brightness.
The Scroll feature should later be separated into its own experimental mode because it is a captured sequence, not a normal color operation.
